## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-08-17T09:51:55Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: practices-discovery
**Details**: Q1=A (trunk-based + squash + monorepo /frontend /backend /contracts + 3 pre-impl contracts: OpenAPI, DB schema+migrations, domain type/enum glossary); Q2=A (walking skeleton first through shared foundation, then vertical slices); Q3=A (BE JUnit5+Spring Boot Test+MockMvc+Mockito, FE RTL+Vitest, Testcontainers PostgreSQL integration, 80% line coverage floor, test-alongside, OpenAPI-based API contract test layer); Q4=A (docker-compose standardized local run, secrets non-committed .env gitignore + .env.example, env/Spring profile injection, CI/CD deferred to impl); Q5=A (FE Prettier+ESLint+TS, BE Spotless+google-java-format+Checkstyle, JSON camelCase + JPA snake_case, no Entity at API boundary, custom {code,message,details} error schema); Q6=A (bcrypt Spring Security default, secrets-non-commit hard rule NEVER, security static analysis SpotBugs+FindSecBugs + eslint-plugin-security, dependency scan npm audit + OWASP Dependency-Check + lockfile pin, anti-dup-account signals salted-hash/masked/retention-window/non-exposed deferred to nfr-requirements/functional-design)
